MA	<p>bd4c_d1e8</p> <p>Restriction enzymes are proteins that cut DNA at specific sequences. <span style="color: #0067cc;"><i>MroI</i></span> is derived from Micrococcus roseus, and <span style="color: #00775f;"><i>SgsI</i></span> is derived from Streptomyces griseus RFL5. Both enzymes are labeled on the map.</p><p>The map shows an intact <strong>12 kb circular DNA molecule</strong>. Each enzyme name marks a restriction site. The 0 kb coordinate is the map origin; it is not necessarily cut by the enzyme in the question.</p><table align="center" border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" role="img" aria-label="Circular restriction-digest DNA map." style="border-collapse: collapse;
